## Costa Rica added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Costa Rica's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **25 books, all of them student books: the five CONED distance-secondary antologías *Antología de Matemáticas*, Niveles 7–11 (UNED/CONED — Nivel Sétimo 2017, Nivel Noveno 2018, Nivel 11 2019; Nivel Décimo by MSc. Jorge Díaz Porras) and the ITCR Proyecto EVEPRIM primary books for Grades 3 and 6 (10 textos + 10 folletos de prácticas across Relaciones y Álgebra, Números, Geometría, Medidas, Estadística and Probabilidad); 0 teacher guides for Costa Rica Maths exist in hand** — and placed 229 teaching items. **SET CORRECTION: the expansion map's count of 17 student is WRONG** — 17 is exactly the number of Costa Rica Mathematics rows in textbooks.tsv (10 EVEPRIM textos + only 2 of the 10 EVEPRIM folletos + 5 CONED antologías); the TSV was never updated for 8 folletos staged on 2026-10-25, and the staging books.json files and phase3-push-state.json agree on the true set of 25 (the folletos count student-side, per the map's own stated basis). All 25 were grabbed, verified (byte size + MD5 against books.json; the two Relaciones y Álgebra textos, which have no books.json entry, verified by size + title page as the main-repo files) and read in full — no partial excerpts, no duplicates (the SharePoint folder copies of the six texto books matched the staged sizes byte-for-byte, so they are the same files, cited once) and no unopenable files. The three Grade 3 textos were read in their November 2026 editions, the in-hand editions per books.json; the GitHub release mid-size-books-3 still holds the superseded October editions of those three textos (a record-level flag, reported, not fixed — same books, older exports; only the November editions are cited here). Two chapter promises in the CONED books are NOT teaching and place no item anywhere: CONED 7's Capítulo 4 is titled Estadística y Probabilidad but teaches statistics only (probability appears only in its introductory discussion questions, pp. 209–214), and CONED 10's Capítulo 1 objectives promise plane transformations that no Tema in the chapter teaches (only a recall of Grade 8 homothety). The placements' named exclusions — front matter, indices, bibliographies and answer keys (soluciones); the EVEPRIM answer-key chapters and the review chapters that re-teach already-placed sections; the CONED chapter motto/introduction pages and “desafío matemático” pages; and the videos and web interactives the EVEPRIM books link to — are apparatus and are stocked nowhere. The 5 CONED book-error groups found in the read — CONED 7: the angle table (p. 106) labels a 360° angle “CONVEXO”, the inverse-proportionality example (pp. 201–203) states v·t = 247.5 for a product of 330 and “very close to 3 hours” at 78 km/h for ≈4.23 h with garbled fraction work, the triangle inequality (p. 125) omits “any two” sides, the median answer (p. 238) gives 68 for a median of 67, the worked powers page (p. 64) misaligns its results, and a tiling exercise (p. 35) reads “largo por largo”; CONED 8: the table of contents numbers the equations Tema “Tema 4” while the body heads it “Tema 5” (numbering slip only); CONED 9: the SI prefix table (p. 74) gives exa the symbol “R” (exa is E), the radian-to-degree rule (p. 117) is printed inverted/garbled though its worked examples are correct, and the contents duplicates “Función Cuadrática” for Capítulo 3 Temas 1 and 4 and mislabels Capítulo 4 as “Capítulo III”; CONED 10: the falling-body model (p. 373) prints “12” for ½ (the worked values use ½ correctly), the key (p. 42) duplicates “tangentes” where “secantes” is meant, p. 14 asks for the area of a “circunferencia” (the circle), and the pentagon solution (p. 86) drops its degree symbols; CONED 11: the logarithm table (p. 118) misaligns its examples (it prints log₅ 5 = 2, which belongs to log₅ 25), the wound-model solution (p. 172) shows an unsigned division display before the correct result, and “invectiva” (p. 121) is a typo for inyectiva — are carried corrected on the rows they touch. One further note is carried as the placements carry it: the Medidas G3 answer key's building-height estimate reads 45 dam in the October copy and “4 dam” in the November extraction — treated as a re-export/extraction artefact in the key, not teaching content, and cited nowhere. Costa Rica's books are pupil books, so Costa Rica is COUNTED: it joins this lesson's acceptance count wherever the placements stock an item. CONED printed page = PDF page throughout; EVEPRIM pages are cited by PDF page with the book's section numbers (two exceptions print folios: the EVEPRIM 6 Probabilidad texto's printed folio = PDF page − 6 and the EVEPRIM 3 Medidas texto's = PDF page − 7; citations give PDF pages). Into this lesson the placements stock 2 items (items 29 and 107).

Pupil textbook.

## Djibouti added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Djibouti's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDF — **1 document, the CRIPEN *Guide de l'enseignant(e) — Mathématiques 5ème année* (teacher's guide, series « À MOI LES MATHS », édition 2023; 129 pages); 0 pupil (student) textbooks** — and placed 68 teaching items. The document is a teacher's guide: for every lesson of the matching pupil manuel it states the headline objective, the pedagogical intention, the pupil objectives, the lesson conduct, the activities and the worked answers. Every Djibouti item in this section is therefore TEACHER-GUIDE EVIDENCE — the guide teaches teachers how to teach the topic; it is never a pupil textbook — cited as the evidence kind it is; Djibouti is counted in NO lesson's taught-in figure, on the standing Ghana evidence-only precedent, until a Djibouti pupil book is in hand and read. No pupil Maths book is in hand: the guide states (printed pp. 9–10) that since the 2019 school year teachers and pupils have a matching pupil manuel and livret of the same series (« À MOI LES MATHS », 5ème année), organised in the same four eight-week modules — those pupil books exist but are not in hand (placements record item R1), the guide's references to the manuel's pages are apparatus, not pupil content read here, and the student Maths files the library records locate are link-only partial extracts, never downloaded; none is part of this read and none is cited. Printed page = PDF page throughout the guide. The placements' counting grain is one item per distinct taught content stated in a lesson sheet's headline objective and pupil objectives, splitting a sheet where it teaches two contents that belong to different course lessons; the pedagogical apparatus around each sheet (situation conduct, activity-by-activity commentary, exercise commentaries and synthesis questions) is not re-counted per activity. The placements' named exclusions — the avant-propos, préambule and présentation (printed pp. 3, 8–12: the competency approach, the four “moments of study”, the teacher's role, evaluation and remediation apparatus), the sommaire itself (printed pp. 4–7; its faults are placements record items R2 and R4), the mental-calculation routines (PLM) opening most sheets, per-sheet conduct and commentary counted once per content, and the guide's cross-references to the pupil manuel and livret pages — are stocked nowhere; the one content-bearing statement in the front matter, the end-of-primary objective's domain list (printed p. 9), is placed as a programme item in the ratio-and-proportion lesson. The Module 2 sheet at printed pp. 55–56 is a near-verbatim reprint of the Module 1 sheet at printed pp. 14–15 (placements record item R3); its content is placed once, as item 1. Into this lesson the placements stock 3 teacher-guide-evidence items from Book 1.

**Book 1:** *Guide de l'enseignant(e) — Mathématiques 5ème année* (teacher guide, Grade 5) — Djibouti, CRIPEN (Ministère de l'Éducation Nationale et de la Formation Professionnelle), édition 2023, series « À MOI LES MATHS »; 1,494,038 bytes, 129 PDF pages. The records' kind labels are wrong for most of the set: 13 of the 18 Mathematics-labelled “student” files are actually teacher-facing (in-service course books, theory texts, activity series and instructor guides), and only 6 files are pupil-facing (the Metsämatikkaa cards, the quadrilateral chart, the weather game, the Päivänselvää chapter, the programming workbook and its model solutions) — every book below is carried at its TRUE kind, never the record label. One byte-identical duplicate (aoe.2674-alku = aoe.2686-alku, the same file under two aoe records) is cited once; the Päivänselvää book is a Chapter-2-only excerpt, cited only for what it holds; the quadrilateral chart's filed copy is 1 content page + 175 blank padding pages. The placements' named exclusions — the weather game's non-temperature content, the computing-pedagogy matter in the two Turku course books, the motivation and mindset material in the JoMa books, the Pikkumatikoita parts 2, 8 and 11, and the programming books' pure-programming tasks — are stocked nowhere. Pupil items are COUNTED normally; teacher-evidence items are named in the rows and stay outside the acceptance count. The Grade 1 Näppituntuma volume's appendices (printed pp. 37–41: number cards 1–100 and a hundred chart, dot cards, and < = > sign cards) are apparatus for its placed items and are stocked nowhere as content. Into this lesson the placements stock 5 pupil items and 5 teacher-evidence items from Book 1, Book 2, Book 3 and Book 4.

TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY — an OPH supplementary teacher activity collection, expressly “not tied to any textbook”; Finland has no national textbook series.

Every book was grabbed fresh by the authenticated GitHub API route, verified byte-exact against its staging `books.json` byte count and cover/title-proved, and the grabbed copy deleted after reading; the recorded Google Drive fallback is login-walled from this host and delivered no book. Checked and NOT an error: *Matemática 4° Secundaria*, printed p. 84, prints Pythagoras as a² = b² + c² with a defined as the hypotenuse — correct as printed. ## Gabon added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Gabon's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **17 documents: 6 pupil textbooks (the CIAM *Mathématiques* volumes for 6e, 5e, 4e, 2e Scientifique, 1re SM and 1re Littéraire, EDICEF), 6 teacher guides (the *Super Efficace* guides pédagogiques for 1re–5e année, EDIG/EDICEF, and the CIAM *Mathématiques 2e S* guide pédagogique) and 5 curriculum documents (the IPN *Curriculum Mathématiques* for 1re–5e année)** — every file grabbed fresh for the read, its byte size and page count matching the records exactly and its identity proved from cover/title/sommaire pages — and placed 522 items: 83 pupil-textbook teaching items, 389 teacher-evidence items and 50 curriculum-requirement items. The expansion map's total of 17 files is right but its split is wrong in kind (the read's flagged claim F1): the map lists “11 student + 6 teacher”, counting the 5 IPN curriculum PDFs among the student books; the true split is 6 pupil textbooks + 6 teacher guides + 5 curriculum documents. **No *Super Efficace* pupil book is in hand at any primary grade** — the primary pupil books are print-only commercial (EDIG) and were never secured, so Gabon's primary coverage is the IPN curriculum plus the teacher guide for each year; each guide teaches every lesson of the absent pupil book and cites its pages, and the guide rows below cite both the guide page and the pupil-book page the guide gives. There are no partial volumes and no duplicates in the set: every CIAM scan runs from its first chapter to its own index at the sommaire's stated page (the 6e scan opens at printed p. 3 — the cover and title leaves are not in the scan, but all teaching content is present, printed pp. 3–222 = the PDF's 220 pages exactly). The two misleading historic Drive filenames are settled by the books' own title pages: the file named “2nde SM” is the CIAM 2e Scientifique pupil book, and the file named “2nde C” is its 2e S Guide pédagogique. Link-only CIAM volumes (3e, Terminale SM/SE and their guides) are not in hand and are cited nowhere. Counting grain: one curriculum item per palier × compétence statement; one guide item per lesson the guide teaches; one pupil item per sommaire chapter. Book errors are carried corrected where they land: the Curriculum 2e année, Compétence 2, Palier 1 prints “le passionnément des objets l'espace”, a typo for “positionnement” (positioning of objects in space), and is stocked corrected; the Curriculum 3e année, Compétence 1, Palier 2 states “nombres de 1001 à 1999 (0 à 1999)” — the parenthetical contradicts the main range, and the statement is stocked by its main range; the CIAM 4e index spells PPMC as “PPCM” (trivial, noted for the record). The placements' named exclusions — the guides' remediation/application apparatus between paliers; the curricula's front matter and framework apparatus (rationale, timetables, competency-framework and assessment sections); the CIAM pupil books' end-of-chapter exercise sets, préfaces, authors' letters and closing indexes; the 2e S pupil book's Greek-alphabet page; the 2e S guide's synoptic programme tables — are stocked nowhere. Gabon stocks only evidence rows in this lesson — teacher-evidence, with no pupil-textbook row — so it is named in the countries line and in the rows but is NOT added to this lesson's acceptance count. ## Fiji added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Fiji's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **13 pupil textbooks of the Ministry of Education / Curriculum Development Unit series, one for each of Years 1–13** — and placed 1,012 items. The expansion map's count is exact: staging `books.json` holds exactly 13 Mathematics entries (one per year, all `kind: student`) and `phase3-push-state.json` lists all 13 as pushed; no Maths teacher guide is in hand (the AQEP Maths Guides for Lower Primary exist only as AnyFlip reader copies in the download list — not in hand, not counted). The staged PDFs had been pushed and deleted locally, so each book was re-fetched for the read — from the repo tree (Contents API) where the pusher had stored it as a blob, else from the book's recorded `source_url` (a Wayback Machine capture of the ministry file, Beacon Media, or Google Drive) — and the grabbed copies were deleted after their placements were written. The read's nine flagged claims ride with the set, honestly: (1) `textbooks.tsv` carries only 9 of the 13 rows (Years 1, 2, 3, 4, 7, 10, 11, 12, 13) — Years 5, 6, 8 and 9 are staged and pushed but missing from the TSV; (2) the Year 2 scan duplicates printed p. 57 and printed p. 58 is absent, so the Money unit's Activity 12 is not in hand to read and is cited nowhere; (3) the Year 5 capture has no cover/title leaves — the file opens at the Strand 1 divider and the body runs complete, Strand 1 p. 1 to Strand 5 p. 98, its identity resting on the staged record and the body's own structure; (4) Year 4's folio sequence is irregular — folios 44–48 and 54 never appear and folios 42 and 55 each appear twice — but the leaf count closes exactly (85 printed numbers − 6 absent + 2 duplicated = 81 body leaves), every contents strand and unit is present and no activity breaks mid-stream at the gaps, so the evidence points to the printed book's own folio misprints rather than missing leaves, a judgement the file alone cannot prove; (5) Year 3's scan order is scrambled at the end (contents folio ii and printed pp. 46, 51, 52 sit at PDF pp. 76–80) and one activity page appears twice (folioed 47 and 51 — counted once), with folio tracking accounting for all of printed pp. 1–75; (6) Year 10 exists in two packagings — the staged Beacon Media file (9,099,595 bytes, 202 pp.) is the copy cited; an ECopies capture of the same book is 9,079,205 bytes / 200 pp.; (7) Year 12's first Wayback transfer arrived truncated (11,794,148 bytes, broken cross-reference table) and was discarded — a fresh download delivered the complete book (17,085,310 bytes, 284 pp.), which is what was read; (8) Year 1's contents lists Chance at p. 65 and Data at p. 66 where the folios are 64 and 65 — the body count closes exactly, so nothing is missing and the contents is one out; (9) Year 6's interior copyright and acknowledgement pages misname it the “Year 7” book — a copy-paste error in the book itself; its cover metadata, contents, running heads and body are Year 6 throughout, and it is read and placed as Year 6. Book errors found in the read are taught corrected, the corrected form being what the items teach — among them Year 3's conversion-table lines “1,000 cm = 1 km” (correct: 100,000 cm) and “10,000 mm = 1,000 m” (correct: 10 m), Year 4's banner “30 minutes = 1 hour” against its own body's 60, Year 5's “1 000 millimetres = 1 litre”, Year 9's contents “Common Multitudes” (for Common Multiples) and its (a × b)ᵐ = aᵐ × bⁿ misprint, and Year 1's count-in-twos strip printing 9 where 19 belongs; Year 10 carries its own printed errata list (pp. 201–202), whose corrections are noted in the items. Counting grain: one item per distinct teaching point — a named concept, method, rule or worked skill with its printed page. The placements' named exclusions — strand divider pages; contents and acknowledgement pages; pure exercise sets and worked-example repeats of an item already placed; Year 1's “Checking my number” ten-frame reference page (printed p. 32); Year 3's duplicated capacity page (counted once); Year 4's closing multiplication-table chart (printed p. 86); Year 10's printed errata list (pp. 201–202, apparatus — its corrections ride in the items); Year 12's closing motivational quotes and exam tips (printed pp. 273–275); Year 13's bibliography and back matter — are stocked nowhere. Fiji COUNTS in this lesson's taught-in figure: every Fiji book is a pupil textbook. Into this lesson the placements stock 17 items, every one a pupil-textbook row, from Book 1 (*Mathematics Year 1*), Book 2 (*Mathematics Year 2*), Book 3 (*Mathematics Year 3*), Book 4 (*Year 5 Mathematics*), Book 5 (*Year 7 Mathematics Textbook*).

Printed page = PDF page − 2. Scan defect: printed p. 57 appears twice (PDF pp. 59–60 are the same leaf) and printed p. 58 is absent — the Money unit's Activity 12 is not in hand and is cited nowhere.

Printed page = PDF page − 3 for the in-sequence body; the scan order is scrambled at the end and the capacity-discovery activity page appears twice (folioed 47 and 51), cited once.

## Honduras added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Honduras's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **21 files = 18 distinct works: 9 pupil textbooks (Grades 3–9, Grade 10 Matemática I and Grade 11 Matemática III BCH) and 12 teacher-guide files for Grades 1, 2, 4, 5, 7, 8, 9, 10 and 11, the Grade 8 guide held in two editions (2018 and the 2022 revision) and the Grade 1 and 2 guides each in two scans** — all from the Secretaría de Educación's PROMETAM / UPNFM series produced with JICA support — and placed 335 items (218 pupil-textbook items and 117 teacher-evidence items), plus 3 duplicate-edition records (placements items 241, 242 and 338) that are cited once with their works and carry no separate items. The expansion map's “9 student + 12 teacher” count is right: the staging `books.json` files, the `phase3-push-state.json` pushed set and `textbooks.tsv` agree on exactly these 21 files. There is no Grade 1–2 pupil textbook in the Secretaría's programme (workbook only — SE Manual de Lineamientos), so the Grade 1–2 teaching in hand is the two guides; there is no Grade 3 or Grade 6 teacher guide in hand. Every file was grabbed from its recorded `source_url` (educatrachos.hn / libroshonduras.com) after the staged PDFs had been pushed and deleted locally, and verified by exact byte size against the download-list record and by page count against the `books.json` record before reading; grabbed copies were deleted after each book's text was extracted. Read basis, stated honestly: the six text-layer pupil books and eight text-layer guides were read in full from their text; the three scanned pupil books (Grades 7–9) were read by OCR of their Índices and unit/lesson openers with interior pages OCR-sampled — Grades 7 and 8 cross-checked against the full text-layer read of their own teacher guides, which plan every lesson class by class — and the two 2022 guides are themselves scans, OCR-read at cover, presentation and index level, the Grade 9 guide therefore placed at unit grain. Counting grain: one item per Índice lesson; a lesson teaching two distinct course topics is split into two items, and three items (a Grade 4 pupil lesson, a Grade 10 pupil lesson and a Grade 2 guide unit) each carry a row in two lessons below and are counted once. The placements' named exclusions — the “Páginas para recortar” cut-out pages of the Grades 3, 4 and 5 pupil books (manipulatives, not teaching text), the Grade 6 book's closing “Repaso” review, and the “Nos divertimos” / “¿Sabías que?” enrichment boxes inside placed lessons — are stocked nowhere. The read's flagged claims ride with the set, honestly: (1) the download list §A's original 2026-10-21 mirror labels for the III-Ciclo files were swapped between Español and Matemáticas — the files it calls “Matemáticas 7/8/9 (2018 PROMETAM)” are the Español books, and its “Español 7/8/9” alternates are the Matemáticas books — a library-record fault, already corrected in `books.json`, reported here and not fixed by this merge; (2) the Grade 9 guide's cover misprints its cycle as “II Ciclo” where the programme is III Ciclo; (3) the Grade 11 pupil book's Índice misprints “Cincunferencias” for “Circunferencias”, taught corrected; (4) the 2014 scan of the Grade 1 guide has a materially poorer text layer than the mirror scan of the same work, so anyone text-mining it alone will under-read it. Book errors found in the read are taught corrected, the corrected form being what the items teach. Into this lesson the placements stock 1 item, every one a pupil-textbook row, from Book 1 (*Libro del Estudiante, Matemáticas, 3er grado*).

**Book 1:** *Libro del Estudiante, Matemáticas, 3er grado* — Honduras, Secretaría de Educación (the PROMETAM / UPNFM series produced with JICA support); 16,407,713 bytes, 202 PDF pp. Printed page = PDF page − 6. Pupil textbook.

The expansion map is WRONG in substance for Mauritius (flag F2): it recorded “1 student / 0 teacher” in hand, but the counted file is a supplementary worksheets excerpt, not a pupil textbook, and the Progress Book was not counted at all; the true in-hand Maths-content set is 0 pupil textbooks, 0 teacher guides, 1 pupil worksheets excerpt and 1 progress-record book. Flag F1: the GitHub record marks the worksheets `quarters: "full"` and the file is named `pupil-full-year.pdf`, but the file itself is 7 PDF pages carrying printed pp. 1–5 only — whatever else Part 1 contains is not in hand, and the excerpt is cited only for the pages actually held (both flags are record-level; the records themselves are not edited — merges never edit the records). All other Mauritius Maths books — the MIE primary and secondary Mathematics textbooks (exact links only) and Prevocational Mathematics Year 2 (ministry link only, extra-grid) — are link-only, not in hand and not read; the other in-hand Mauritius files (Road Safety Education Grades 1–6, *A Pictorial Primer — Say No to Tobacco*, Kreol Morisien textbooks, the Pre-Primary curriculum documents) are other subjects, verified by a full census of the Mauritius folders. The placements' named exclusions — Book A's front matter (cover, Numeracy Panel page, copyright); the Grade 7 colour-naming competency (no mathematics content as stated; the pattern competency it prepares for is counted at Grade 8); the Grade 9 “perform arithmetic operations mentally” process statement; the incomplete printed fragment “including algebraic fractions ( )” at Grade 9+; the Progress Book's non-Mathematics subject sections and its blank record apparatus; and record items R1–R3 (a Physical Education cross-reference, the front-matter GPA worked example, and the worksheets cover's external game link) — are stocked nowhere. The Progress Book's book errors are carried corrected on the rows they touch: the Grade 7 fraction list prints ½ twice (item 17); the H.C.F./L.C.M. word-problem requirement is stated a second time, misplaced inside §1.6 Decimals (item 94); three statements are truncated in the book — “involving brackets of the form”, the form never given (item 145), “a^n for n = −1, −2, −3 and rational” (item 153), and “or in the form” (item 216); §5.2 is headed “Pictograms and bar charts” but states only frequency-table work (item 151); π is printed as the Cyrillic letter “л” (item 172); and the quadratic rows are printed under the §3.5 Matrices heading with the general form garbled as “( a 2+b +c)”, read as ax² + bx + c (items 199–202). Mauritius's class for Maths is **PARTIAL (evidence-heavy)**: only the 10 taught items rest on pupil material, and that from a 5-page excerpt, so Mauritius is named in the countries line and in the rows below but is added to NO lesson's acceptance count, on the Bahamas/Canada evidence precedent. ## Iceland added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Iceland's whole in-hand Maths set from the files — **28 files: 14 pupil books and 14 teacher books**, all published by MMS (Miðstöð menntunar og skólaþjónustu, formerly Námsgagnastofnun), the Icelandic state publisher; the Sproti (Grades 1–4) and Stika (Grades 5–7) series are Icelandic adaptations of the Norwegian *Multi* series (Gyldendal), and Skali is the Icelandic adaptation of the Norwegian *Maximum* series — and placed 229 items (161 pupil + 68 teacher evidence). The expansion map's count and kind split are exact. Three record matters ride with the set (record-level; the records themselves are not edited — merges never edit the records): textbooks.tsv omits the Stika 1a Kennarabók (27 TSV rows for the true 28 files — the book itself is in hand, in books.json, the push-state and the GitHub release); no Stika 1b Kennarabók exists in any record, although the Stika 1a pupil book's own front matter lists kennarabækur 1a and 1b as series components, so Stika 1b pupil content stands on the pupil book alone; and Sproti 4b's secured PDF (154 pp.) files its Ch. 9 (Mælingar og tugabrot) twice — PDF pp. 50–71 and PDF pp. 72–93 are the same printed chapter — counted once in every citation below. The set holds only the Skali 1A teacher book for Grade 8 — no Skali pupil book is in hand — so every Skali item is teacher evidence. The placements' named exclusions are stocked nowhere: the workbooks (æfingahefti) and worksheet files, which exist only as download-list links and are not in the in-hand set; the Skali pupil books (1A/1B nemendabækur), not in hand; the other MMS series in the download list (Stærðfræði 8+/9+/10+, Vinkill, Áttatíu), not in hand and not read; and front matter, the books' SPIL game pages as standalone content, and the Norwegian-original credits, none counted as items (the pupil books' own built-in practice pages and tests are apparatus inside the books read). ## Myanmar added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Myanmar's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **2 documents, the Ministry of Education *Teacher Guide Mathematics-1, Grade 6* and *Teacher Guide Mathematics-2, Grade 6* (Basic Education Curriculum, Syllabus and Textbook Committee, 2019-2020; Mathematics-1: 133 PDF pages; Mathematics-2: 99 PDF pages); 0 pupil (student) textbooks** — and placed 114 teaching items. Each guide carries the full lesson plans for its half of the Grade 6 year — learning outcomes, lesson objectives, the taught subject matter with worked examples and answers, practice and review; Mathematics-1 covers the number / algebra / statistics / social-mathematics half of the year (Chapters 1–9) and Mathematics-2 the geometry / mensuration half (Chapters 1–10); the two files are two halves of one complete Grade 6 teacher-guide slot, not duplicates of each other and not parts of one file. Every Myanmar item in this section is therefore TEACHER-GUIDE EVIDENCE — the guides teach teachers how to teach the topic; neither book is a pupil textbook — cited as the evidence kind it is; Myanmar is counted in NO lesson's taught-in figure, on the standing Ghana evidence-only precedent, until a Myanmar pupil book is in hand and read. No pupil Maths book is in hand: both guides' lesson plans follow a matching Grade 6 pupil textbook (the guides refer throughout to “the textbook”, its figures, examples and exercises — e.g. Mathematics-1 exercises 1.1–9.8); that pupil textbook exists but is not in hand (placements record item R1) — the guides' references to it are apparatus, not pupil content read here — and every Myanmar student Maths book the library records locate is an exact-link record only, never downloaded; none is part of this read and none is cited. The expansion map's count and kind split are exact (0 student + 2 teacher): textbooks.tsv carries exactly 8 Myanmar rows, all Grade 6 teacher guides, exactly two of them Mathematics; neither push-state names Myanmar (the books were secured pre-phase-3 into the textbooks repo's main tree) and phase3-staging holds no Myanmar directory, so the read copies were grabbed by the authenticated GitHub API route at the exact recorded byte sizes (Mathematics-1: 1,117,915 bytes; Mathematics-2: 1,312,022 bytes) and deleted after the read. Printed page = PDF page throughout both guides (the folio printed at the head of every page equals its PDF page). The placements' counting grain is one item per distinct taught content stated in a lesson plan's objective and teaching section, splitting a lesson where it teaches two contents that belong to different course lessons; the guides' shared pedagogy apparatus (student-centred method, Bloom's taxonomy, assessment, the I-T-P-R lesson stages, per-lesson introduction/practice/review conduct) is not counted per lesson, and exercise sets drawn from the matching pupil textbook are apparatus serving the lesson's own item, not separate content. The placements' named exclusions — the shared pedagogy Introduction in both guides (printed pp. 3–14 of each: student-centred method, basic rules for learning activity, Bloom's taxonomy, questioning stages, classroom assessment, 21st-century/soft skills and classroom social aspects; the syllabus tables inside it are placements record item R2 — the programme frame for the lesson plans, not separate taught content, and no syllabus-only topic was found that no lesson plan teaches), the I-T-P-R lesson-stage apparatus, entry-exit checks, group-work conduct and learning-journal directions repeated in every lesson plan, the exercise numbers cited from the pupil textbook (a book not in hand), and the glossary both prefaces announce, which is carried in the textbook and stands in neither guide as printed (recorded as an absence, verified on the read) — are stocked nowhere. The placements' record items R3–R4 (Mathematics-1's contents skips section 1.4 in its numbering although the body teaches 1.4 Distributive Property at printed pp. 26–27, and lists section 5.3 twice for the body's two successive 5.3 lessons, placed once as item 49; Mathematics-2's contents heads Chapter 9 without its chapter-number line although the body heads it “Chapter 9 Mensuration (1)” at printed p. 79) are contents slips, stocked nowhere. The placements' book errors E1–E8 are taught corrected, each noted on the row it touches. Into this lesson the placements stock 2 teacher-guide-evidence items from Book 1.

## Nauru added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Nauru's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **10 documents, all of them Learning Through Doing (LTD) *Mathematics Planning Documents*, Nauru editions (2026) — Prep and Years 1–8, plus the LTD *Scope and Sequence, Years 1–8* — teacher planning alignments that sequence an Australian commercial subscription lesson system against the Nauru Mathematics Syllabus, every lesson keyed to a Nauru curriculum code (NRU-*); 0 pupil (student) textbooks, 0 teacher guides, 0 workbooks** — and placed 238 items, every one of them PLANNING/CURRICULUM EVIDENCE: the Nauru syllabus's own key-idea statements as printed on each document's closing “Nauru Curriculum Alignment” pages. Nauru publishes no government textbooks (country record, download list §§F–Q, seven fronts), so Nauru's standing in the course is the syllabus-evidence reckoning: Nauru is counted in NO lesson's taught-in figure and is named in the countries line and in the rows below only, on the standing evidence-only precedent (Bahamas / Canada / Myanmar). The expansion map's count is right in number and wrong in kind: it carries Nauru as “10 student Maths books”, but every one of the 10 books.json entries is kind `planning-document` and states in its own approval note that it is NOT a Nauru government student textbook (diff fault F1 — a record-side fault, reported, not fixed by this merge). textbooks.tsv carries 12 Nauru rows, all of them USP IOE ESD items, and zero Mathematics rows — the 10 planning documents live only in books.json and push-state. Australia overlap is ZERO: the Australia read's nine Cambridge/Jacaranda Years 8–10 pupil textbooks share no title, ISBN, byte size or file with this set, and the QCE senior titles cross-referenced in Nauru's country record are link-only, in hand for neither country and read by neither reader; nothing from the Australia placements is cited here. All 10 files were read from local disk in phase3-staging, byte-exact against their books.json records (334,327–887,725 bytes; 67 PDF pages in total; 10 distinct MD5s, no duplicates inside the set); nothing was downloaded. Printed-page basis: the documents carry no printed folios (the Scope's footer “Page N” equals its PDF page), so pages cited are document (PDF) pages — Prep key ideas on alignment pp. 6–7 of 7, Years 1–8 on alignment p. 6 of 6; the term overviews (p. 1) and weekly plans (pp. 2–5) sequence the same key ideas and add none of their own. Counting grain: one item per NRU key idea. The *Scope and Sequence* (887,725 bytes, 13 PDF pages) was read in full and cross-checked — every Scope topic traces to a year document's lessons under the codes placed — so it contributes 0 items of its own; counting it again would double-count one programme as two sources. Book errors found on the read are placed corrected, as the placements record them (diff fault F2): Year 3 NRU-M3.3 prints “suing” for “using” and NRU-G3.3 prints “jey” for “key”; Year 5 NRU-CD5.4 prints “recoding sheets” for “recording sheets”; Year 8 NRU-PA8.4 prints “co-ordiinates”; the Year 8 alignment page prints its first geometry code as “NRU-G9.1” — a Year 9 code inside the Year 8 document — while the Year 8 weekly plans cite the same lessons as NRU-G8.1, and item 226 is placed as the Year 8 item it is; the Year 2 document's Term 4 plan cites “NRU-N3.4” against Year 2 strategy-review lessons, where the alignment page's NRU-N2.4 governs; several weekly-plan code strings are garbled in print — the alignment pages' code lists are clean and govern throughout. The placements also record a document inconsistency (diff fault F3): the Scope's fractions table opens its halves entry at the earliest (Prep) level, but the Prep planning document contains no fractions anywhere — fractions enter the year documents at Year 1 (NRU-N1.8) — and the placements follow the year documents. Some key ideas are explicitly Nauru-localised (Year 3 NRU-G3.3: simple maps of Nauru; Year 6 NRU-CD6.4: data contexts “familiar to Nauruan students”; Year 8 NRU-PA8.6: graphs “using data from Nauru”). The placements' named exclusions are stocked nowhere: the Nauru Mathematics Syllabus aims paragraph printed in every year document (general aims, no subject content); the term overviews and weekly plans as separate items (they sequence the same key ideas, and the LTD lesson titles are the Australian system's commercial catalogue, delivered by subscription — the planning documents do not reproduce the lessons, so no lesson content is placed, only the syllabus key ideas the lessons are sequenced against); “Assessment Opportunities” headers and blank assessment columns (apparatus); the Scope & Sequence's duplicated topic statements across years (one programme, counted once at key-idea grain); the 12 USP IOE ESD files filed under Nauru (not Mathematics, excluded by subject); and the OER4Pacific COL open-schooling Mathematics 11/12 PDFs named in the download list §G (regional OER, never staged under Nauru, not in hand, not read). Into this lesson the placements stock 2 planning/curriculum-evidence items from Book 1 (*LTD Mathematics Planning Document, Prep, Nauru edition (2026)*), Book 2 (*LTD Mathematics Planning Document, Year 1, Nauru edition (2026)*).

## Jamaica added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Jamaica's whole in-hand Maths set from the files — **8 documents: 7 National Standards Curriculum (NSC) guides that carry a Mathematics section (Grades 1, 2 and 3 Integrated Studies / Language Arts / Mathematics; Grades 4, 5 and 6 Exploratory Core; and the Grades 7–9 Mathematics guide — all Ministry of Education, Youth and Information, © 2018, the Grade 6 guide in its FINAL edition of Jan 08 2020), plus 1 pupil reader** — and placed 311 items: 308 curriculum-evidence items from the guides and 3 pupil-text items from the reader. **Kind split: 0 pupil textbooks, 0 teacher guides, 7 curriculum guides.** A curriculum guide states what is REQUIRED to be taught and how to teach it; it is not a pupil textbook, so every guide item below is CURRICULUM EVIDENCE, worded “curriculum requires”, and Jamaica is counted in NO lesson's taught-in figure. The expansion map is wrong on both count and kind (flag F1, record-side, reported not fixed): it listed Jamaica as FILES-IN-HAND with 4 student / 0 teacher Maths books — its build matched the subject field only, counting the three Grades 1–3 guides and the Grades 7–9 guide and missing the three Grades 4–6 Exploratory Core guides, whose subject field reads only “Exploratory Core” while their title pages name Mathematics as one of the four core subjects; and the four files it did count are curriculum guides, not student books. Two further record faults ride with the set (record-side; the records themselves are not edited — merges never edit the records, and no maths/books/jamaica.md filing exists or was created): textbooks.tsv has **no row at all for the Grades 7–9 Mathematics guide** (flag F2), although the file is in staging, in books.json and in the pushed set; and the TSV's Grade 5 row points at a Telegram link instead of the file's secured GitHub asset (flag F3) — the book was read byte-exact from that asset. One file defect, stated plainly: the Grade 5 guide's body font has a broken ToUnicode map, so its text layer extracts as bullet glyphs; its Mathematics section was read in full from rendered page images (120-DPI renders of PDF pp. 139–255 = printed pp. 122–238), every page legible, and it is counted as read on that basis. Printed-page basis: each guide prints its own folio; the Mathematics windows run printed = PDF − 5 (Grade 1), − 33 (Grade 2), − 16 (Grade 3), − 11 (Grade 4), − 17 (Grade 5) and − 12 (Grade 6), and Book 7's pages are its own per-grade section pages. Counting grain: Grades 1–3, one item per distinct numbered objective in the Mathematics Content Standards, plus NEW/Extended objectives and Scope-and-Sequence topics stated in no standard (marked (S&S)); Grades 4–6 and 7–9, one item per distinct objective in each Mathematics unit's objectives list; an objective restated verbatim is counted once, at its first full statement. Boundary files examined and excluded — stocked nowhere: the nursery *Birth to Three is Key* early-childhood guide (ages 0–3, all-domains; number concepts are one activity focus, with no Mathematics section or objectives at NSC grain — not a Maths book); the Grades 4–6 Resource & Technology guide (its file is a bundle whose opening leaves are the Grades 7–9 English guide; no Mathematics section); and the Civics (×2), English Language, Integrated Science and Social Studies guides (no Mathematics subject in any of them; the Science guide's measurement/graphing is science content). The placements' named exclusions inside the books are likewise stocked nowhere: the Grade 1 window's “Maths Prayers” (printed pp. 242–243 — devotional poems, religious apparatus, no mathematics content); the Scope and Sequence charts, Process Strand checklists, Illustrative Activities, Benchmarks and Illustrative Examples as apparatus (topics stated only there are placed as items and marked (S&S)); the Integrated Studies and Language Arts windows of the Grades 1–3 guides, which restate the same Mathematics attainment targets as delivery vehicles and state no requirement beyond the window's standards; the Grade 1 book's Mathematics lesson plans and “Teaching Mathematics: Ideas & Activities” (printed pp. 340–400, teacher apparatus); Book 7's Appendices A–E and closing sample lesson plan (printed pp. 191–206, activity apparatus and worked examples of the same objectives); and the reader's copyright/credits leaves. Book errors and faults are carried as the placements record them, taught corrected where they touch a row: the Grade 1 fractions standard (printed p. 275) cross-refers an objective as “Objective 2.3.27” — a Grade 2 bank number inside the Grade 1 book — and objective numbers with a leading “2.” recur throughout the Grade 1 standards (the bank numbering is shared across grades; each such objective is placed once, as stated for Grade 1); the Grade 2 addition example on printed p. 268 (“Pat has 43 oranges … How many apples Pat has now?”) mixes fruits mid-problem — a wording slip, the objective it illustrates placed as read; two Grade 2 standards pages carry a mojibake line (a Caesar-shifted “See previous page for Attainment Targets and Objectives”) — a print-production artefact, no content lost; the Grade 3 window and its Scope and Sequence state **no decimals** anywhere, so no Grade 3 decimals row is stocked; the Grade 4 Term 3 Unit 2 outcome names “cross-sections” of cubes/cuboids, which the unit nowhere teaches — placed inside its item as named-only; the Grade 4 probability unit's header prints “GRAGE 4” (sic); the Grade 5 Overview disagrees with its own units three times, recorded as printed (division of five-digit numbers sits in Term 3 Unit 1, not Term 2; the Overview's Volume has no objective anywhere in the Grade 5 Mathematics section and is stocked nowhere; lines of symmetry sit in Term 2 Unit 3, while the Term 3 Geometry unit is circles, polygons to eight sides, and pyramids); the Grade 6 Term 3 Unit 1 tyre-pressure activity is Science-linked and its arithmetic is already inside its unit's item, not placed separately; in Book 7, the Grade 9 ratio unit's footer prints “NSC Mathematics: Grade 7” (a template error — the unit sits in the Grade 9 section and is placed as Grade 9), the matrices unit header prints “GRADE 9 TERM 1: NUMBERS” although the unit sits in the Term 3 sequence (placed as Term 3), the Grade 7 constructions unit's objectives page repeats the transformations unit's first objective (counted once, in the transformations item), and the Grade 8 Term 2 geometry unit carries a header title copied from the algebra unit while its objectives are polygons and angle sums (placed by its objectives). Jamaica's pupil Maths textbooks are commercial (Carlong / Hodder / Macmillan etc.) — the library holds none; they stand in the download list only, not in hand and not read. Into this lesson the placements stock 8 curriculum-evidence rows from Book 1, Book 2 and Book 3.

**Book 1:** *Grade 1 NSC Guide: Integrated Studies | Language Arts | Mathematics* (curriculum guide) — Jamaica, Ministry of Education, Youth and Information (MoEYI), © 2018; 12,050,591 bytes, 401 PDF pp., MD5 3f0ff2b594e25bbdaad9f24bf588ec2d. Mathematics window printed pp. 241–307; printed p. = PDF p. − 5. CURRICULUM EVIDENCE ONLY — the guide states the Content Standards a Grade 1 pupil must reach; it is not a pupil textbook, and its rows are counted in no lesson's taught-in figure.

**Book 2:** *Grade 2 NSC Guide: Integrated Studies | Language Arts | Mathematics* (curriculum guide) — Jamaica, Ministry of Education, Youth and Information (MoEYI), © 2018; 18,836,050 bytes, 403 PDF pp., MD5 ac6b122211099d18c2f972dadb29bd97. Mathematics window printed pp. 234–296; printed p. = PDF p. − 33. CURRICULUM EVIDENCE ONLY — the same standing as Book 1.

**Book 3:** *Grade 3 NSC Guide: Integrated Studies | Language Arts | Mathematics* (curriculum guide) — Jamaica, Ministry of Education, Youth and Information (MoEYI), © 2018; 22,514,507 bytes, 474 PDF pp., MD5 1be3a4158691656aa6d81c9635ef2846. Mathematics window printed pp. 299–379; printed p. = PDF p. − 16. CURRICULUM EVIDENCE ONLY. No decimals are stated anywhere in this window or its Scope and Sequence, and no Grade 3 decimals row is stocked from it.

Jamaica enters this lesson as curriculum evidence only: it is named in the countries line and in the rows above, but it is NOT added to the acceptance count above — Jamaica's in-hand Maths set holds no pupil textbook and no teacher guide, only the seven National Standards Curriculum guides, which state what must be taught without being pupil teaching material themselves (the standing evidence-only precedent). Jamaica's class for Maths is PARTIAL (evidence-only): every in-hand document has been read in full and stocked, and it advances only when a Jamaica pupil Maths textbook lands in hand and is read.

## Serbia added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Serbia's whole in-hand Maths set from the file — **one pupil textbook**: *Математика 1* (Matematika 1 — Mathematics for the first grade of primary school), Нова школа (Nova škola), Belgrade, first edition 2018 — and placed **95 teaching items** from it. The book is a genuine, complete pupil textbook (уџбеник), not a workbook, not an exam paper and not an excerpt: every topic is presented, named and worked before it is practised. It is the only Serbia Maths book in hand — the expansion map's count and kind split (1 student, 0 teacher) are exact, and every record agrees. The file was read visually from full-page renders, every page, because its text layer is empty (a print-image PDF). It is the COMPLETE book: the CIP record gives 103 pages, the contents lists all seven units ending at printed p. 103, and the text runs printed pp. 1–103 followed by the imprint page (PDF p. 104). Printed-page basis: **printed page = PDF page** for the whole body, verified against printed folios throughout. Counting grain: one item per distinct taught skill or concept, at the point the book teaches it. The book's revision spreads (ОВО ЗНАМ / НАУЧИЛИ СМО) and its practice set (ЗАДАЦИ ЗА ВЕЖБАЊЕ) restate taught items and are named exclusions — except three places where a revision spread is the book's first presentation of new material (the number line and capacity-by-pouring on printed pp. 48–49, and balance-equivalence on printed p. 82), which are counted as teaching items and marked as such; in-lesson exercise sets are apparatus, not items. Also stocked nowhere: the front cover, contents, the seven unit divider pages and the imprint page, and the road-safety asides on printed p. 11 (no maths content). No book error was found in the book: its worked examples check out, and the contents page numbers agree with the printed folios throughout — a sheet-scale reading of printed p. 73 that seemed to omit 12 from the list of the numbers of the second ten was disproved on full-resolution re-check (the list is complete, 11, 12, 13, …, 20), an artefact of the reading scale, not a book error. Boundary, cited nowhere: Serbia's other 16 Maths files in the Kavčić free library (Grades 1–8 workbooks, textbooks and part-volumes, including the Grade 1 workbook *Математика 1 — радна свеска*) are link-only, not in hand and not read. One record-side note (reported, not fixed — merges never edit the records, and no maths/books/serbia.md filing exists or was created): textbooks.tsv secures only 6 of the 7 pushed Serbia books — the Grade 4 Serbian grammar book is in books.json and in the push state but has no TSV row; it is not a maths book and does not affect this reckoning. Into this lesson the placements stock 7 rows — all from Book 1.

**Book 1:** *Математика 1* (Matematika 1 — Mathematics for the first grade of primary school) (pupil textbook) — Serbia, Нова школа (Nova škola), Belgrade, first edition 2018; authors Милица Ћук, Бранислав Милошевић, Бранко Марковић; ISBN 978-86-6225-041-4; ministry approval решение 650-02-00208/2018-07 of 27. 4. 2018; 135,143,758 bytes, 104 PDF pp., MD5 9a5df48784a319517cff29b5b407d0b2. Complete book: printed pp. 1–103 + covers and imprint; printed page = PDF page throughout. Read visually from full-page renders (the file's text layer is empty). PUPIL TEXTBOOK — Serbia is counted in this lesson's taught-in figure.

## Haiti added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Haiti's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **28 files, every one of them teacher-side**: the nine *Programme à Compétences Minimales* (PCM) volumes for Années Fondamentales 1–9 (MENFP, Direction de l'Enseignement Fondamental, November 2019), the three IPN *Programme détaillé, Matières de base* volumes (4e, 5e, 6e année), the three *Programme Pédagogique Opérationnel* (PPO) Mathématiques fascicules for the 3e cycle (7e, 8e, 9e année, 1989-1990), the eight Nectar (MENFP) teacher sequences (four for 7e année, four for 8e année), the Nouveau Secondaire *Programme-Cadre* and the four NS detailed programmes (NS1–NS4) — every one of the 28 carrying a full Mathématiques section — and placed **1,639 items, every one of them TEACHER/CURRICULUM EVIDENCE**: **Haiti has no pupil Maths textbook in hand under any label**, so Haiti is counted in NO lesson's taught-in figure and is named in the countries line and in the rows below only, on the standing evidence-only precedent (Ghana / Bahamas / Canada / Myanmar). The expansion map's count for Haiti is wrong: it carries 15, counting only the books.json entries whose subject field reads “Mathematics”, and the download list claims 43 teacher files; the true maths-bearing set is 28 files — the nine “All subjects” PCM volumes, the three IPN detailed programmes and the NS Programme-Cadre all carry full maths sections the count missed (a record-side fault, reported, not fixed by this merge); textbooks.tsv is likewise a stale 28-row index naming only a subset of the secured files. Haiti's official pupil books (the MENFP/FAGEM *Manuels uniques* / “Mon manuel” series) are in hand for other subjects only, never for Mathematics, and the two remanié programme leads (a 7e année fascicule and a 4e année detailed programme) are not in hand — and programme documents are not textbooks under the 2026-11-10 textbook-test ruling in any case — so **Haiti stays PARTIAL for Maths, teacher evidence only**. All 28 files were read page-by-page from the secured PDFs in phase3-staging, byte-exact against their books.json records; nothing was downloaded. Read depth: the PPO 8e fascicule and the IPN 4e and 6e programmes are image-only scans and were read by OCR in full, spot-checked against the page images — where OCR garbled a worked example's digits, the placement teaches the corrected arithmetic and the case is flagged; PPO 7e PDF p. 48 and PPO 9e PDF pp. 20, 30, 31 and 32 were verified blank, separator or title pages carrying no objectives. Printed-page bases: PCM printed page = PDF page − 1; IPN 4e and 6e printed page = PDF page − 9, IPN 5e printed folios equal PDF pages; PPO 7e printed = PDF − 3, PPO 8e printed folio = PDF page, PPO 9e printed = PDF − 2; Programme-Cadre NS printed = PDF − 9 (verified against the detailed programme at §5.1–§5.2); NS1 and NS2 printed = PDF page, NS3 cites PDF pages, NS4 printed = PDF − 1; the Nectar sequences cite PDF pages by séance. The placements' eleven flags are all document-side and are carried here exactly: the PCM 6e volume's maths section header mislabels its level “5ème AF” (F1 — its items are cited to the 6e volume they stand in); PCM 5e's spoken-number row slips (“six mille deux” for 6,002, and a thousands-family slip in item 352's row) are placed corrected (F2); the PCM “orthocentre” definition error stands in the PCM **7e** volume — the diff's “(AF8)” label is the diff's own slip, disproven against the live PCM 8e section, which carries the correct circumcentre definition (F3); PCM 9e's right-asymptote and strict-inequality rows are completions of points the volume leaves silent, not errors (F4); PCM 9e's opening “années diagnostiques” ((6e), 7e, 8e) reinstate the diagnostic year the 3e-cycle fascicules dropped (F5); the PCM 7e volume announces a simple-interest objective that does not exist anywhere in its grids — nothing is placed for it, and simple interest is taught by PCM 9e and PPO 9e (F6); the Nectar “Relations entre les angles” sequence states a false corresponding-angles criterion and a mise en train with no solution, and the “Calcul littéral” sequence's séance 5 example drops a factor of x — all placed as corrected (F7, F8); the record-side faults (the map's count, the download list's count, the stale textbooks.tsv) are reported, not fixed (F9); the IPN 6e worked examples carry numerical inconsistencies (objectives 5.1, 7.1, 12.2, 16.1, 19.1, 21.1, 22.1) flagged as read and NOT asserted as print errors — the volume is an OCR-read scan and some may be digit misreads (F10); and NS2's own dating (2015 in the detailed programmes' shared headers, the school year 2008-2009 on its cover) is the book's own, not a discrepancy (F11). The placements' named exclusions are stocked nowhere: the PCM volumes' other-discipline sections and their week-by-week scheduling columns beyond each distinct objective; the IPN volumes' maths avant-propos, theme indexes, other-subject sections and Suggestions/Évaluation apparatus (except where an activity is itself the taught content); the PPO fascicules' shared front matter, Programme-Cadre theme tables, progression grids, bibliographies and annexes; the Nectar sequences' summative-evaluation séances and session-management apparatus (except where a séance's activity is the taught content); the Programme-Cadre's other subjects, and the Nouveau Secondaire framework chapters shared between it and the NS1/NS2 volumes (placed once, from the Programme-Cadre volume); and the verified blank and separator pages. Into this lesson the placements stock 21 items — all teacher/curriculum-evidence rows — from Book 1 (*PCM, 1ère AF (MENFP, 2019)*), Book 2 (*PCM, 2ème AF (MENFP, 2019)*), Book 3 (*PCM, 3ème AF (MENFP, 2019)*), Book 4 (*PCM, 4e AF (MENFP, 2019)*), Book 5 (*PCM, 5e AF (MENFP, 2019)*), Book 6 (*PCM, 6e AF (MENFP, 2019)*), Book 7 (*PCM, 7e AF (MENFP, 2019)*), Book 8 (*IPN Programme détaillé, 4e année*), Book 9 (*IPN Programme détaillé, 5e année*), Book 10 (*IPN Programme détaillé, 6e année*) and Book 11 (*PPO Mathématiques, 7e année (1989-1990)*).

## Suriname added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Suriname's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **18 books: 13 pupil Maths books, 3 teacher Maths books and 2 kindergarten curriculum books** (20 files examined; two further in-hand files, the LJ3 OJW *Leerlingenwerkboek* and *Ik-boek*, were checked and excluded — drawing and orientation activities with no rekenen strand and no number teaching) — and placed **376 items: 344 pupil teaching items, 7 teacher-evidence items and 25 curriculum-evidence items**. No Suriname row existed in any maths lesson before this merge, so every placed item is new here. **Set verdict, proved from the books themselves:** the expansion map's count is right for the Mathematics-labelled set (13 pupil + 3 teacher = 16 files), but its kind labels are wrong in three places: (1) the LJ6 “Leerkrachtenhandleiding Deel 1” of the records is the programme's **Algemene Inleiding** volume (Book 14), not a lesson-by-lesson guide to Deel 1; (2) the two LJ7 teacher books are **oplossingenboeken — answer keys** (Books 15–16), teacher evidence; (3) Book 4 is a **rekenboek** (lesson book), not the “Leerlingenboek” textbooks.tsv names it — the same book. **Maths was hiding under another subject label once:** the KG *Spelend leren* Themaboek 3 and 4 (Books 17–18), filed as “Play-based learning” with textbooks.tsv kind “student” — also wrong: they are **teacher curriculum books** — each carry twelve *voorbereidend rekenen* (preparatory maths) sections with stated learning goals; their 24 items are stocked as **curriculum evidence**, never pupil teaching. **Partial volumes, named as such:** Books 4 and 5 are **Deel A only** and Book 12 is **Deel 1 only**; the download list's remark that LJ8 Deel 2 is “already held” is **not borne out by any record in hand** (textbooks.tsv, the staging books.json and the push-state carry only Deel 1 for LJ8) — LJ8 Deel 2 is treated as not in hand, and the LJ4/LJ5 B-volumes and LJ8 Deel 2–3 are link-only or unrecorded, cited nowhere. Book 8 (LJ6 Deel 3) is staged and pushed but has no textbooks.tsv row — the map counted it, the TSV does not carry it. **The six flagged claims are carried exactly:** (F1) **Book 11 (LJ7 Deel 3) has no Les 24** — contents and body both jump from Les 23 (tangram) to Les 25 (tekenen op schaal); the book holds 38 lessons, not 39, and the rows cite the 38 that exist; (F2) the LJ8-Deel-2 “already held” claim, above — contradicted, not in hand; (F3) **book error, Book 6 Les 6 (printed p. 23):** the information block adds a quarter hour to 15.45 and gives the result as “06:00” — the correct result is **16:00**, and the row teaches the corrected form; (F4) **book error, Book 7 Les 14 (printed p. 54):** the information states the area of a rectangle with a garbled formula line (the perimeter formula 2l + 2b is given correctly, and the area is worked by counting in the same lesson) — the row teaches perimeter = 2l + 2b and area by counting/calculation, the forms the lesson's own worked examples use; (F5) **book error, Book 6 Les 30 (printed p. 93):** the split-subtraction check line prints minus signs where the method needs the parts added (7,986 − 5,234 split as 2,000 + 700 + 50 + 2 = 2,752) — the method itself is taught correctly in Les 21 and the rows follow the correct form; (F6) **Book 13's kind:** textbooks.tsv files the LJ10 Reader as a pupil book; its foreword states it is a guide “voor de reken-leerkracht en leerling” (for teacher and pupil) — its items are placed as teaching content, worded as a **programme reader**, not a pupil textbook. **Text-layer caveat (not a claim):** Book 3's text layer drops the digit “1” in several headings (“splitsingen van 0” for the splitting of 10); the rows use the true values, read from the sums and context. **Checked and NOT flagged:** the answer keys (Books 15–16) match their pupil books lesson for lesson (39 and 38 lessons respectively), and Book 14's contents listing of the kwartaal-1 lessons matches Book 6. Byte sizes checkable against the gov.sr records matched exactly (Books 7, 8, 10, 11). Printed-page basis: printed page = PDF page in every book except Books 17–18 (printed = PDF − 5). The placements' named exclusions are stocked nowhere: the *Vooruit* books' week-closing revision pages (*weekafsluitingen*); Books 1–3's *herhaling* revision worksheets, *oefentoets* practice tests, *knipbladen* cut-out sheets and automatisation games, except where a sheet first teaches a point (those are placed); the *Plezier* books' closing games; Book 13's ch. 1 programme tables beyond item R1; and Books 17–18's non-maths theme sections. The *Plezier in rekenen* companion volumes found by the 2026-11-10 gov.sr census (LJ4 B leerlingenboek, LJ4 werkboek/takenboek, LJ5 takenboek/bijwerkboek) are link-only finds, not in hand — not read, not counted. Suriname counts in a lesson's taught-in figure only on the strength of its pupil rows; its teacher rows and curriculum rows are evidence — cited as the evidence kind they are and never the basis of the count (the standing Ghana evidence precedent). Into this lesson the placements stock 12 items (10 pupil, 2 curriculum evidence) from Book 1 (*Plezier in rekenen, Leerlingenwerkboek 1 (LJ3)*), Book 2 (*Plezier in rekenen, Leerlingenwerkboek 2 (LJ3)*), Book 3 (*Plezier in rekenen, Leerlingenwerkboek 3 (LJ3)*), Book 4 (*Plezier in rekenen, rekenboek A (LJ4)*), Book 5 (*Plezier in rekenen, lessenboek 5a (LJ5)*), Book 6 (*Vooruit met Rekenen, Deel 1 (LJ6)*), Book 9 (*Vooruit met Rekenen, Deel 1 (LJ7)*), Book 17 (*Spelend leren, Themaboek 3 (KG)*) and Book 18 (*Spelend leren, Themaboek 4 (KG)*).

## Papua New Guinea added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Papua New Guinea’s whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **32 files = 28 works, all Papua New Guinea Department of Education (NDoE), “Issued free to schools by the Department of Education”: 8 pupil textbook files (7 works — the JICA/QUIS-ME National Mathematics Textbooks, Grades 1–6, with Grades 3 and 5 in two parts each) and 24 teacher files (21 works — 9 JICA teacher’s manual files, 6 works with Grades 3–5 in two parts each, and 15 Standards Based Curriculum (SBC) teacher guides, Elementary Prep to Grade 12, with separate Advanced and General guides at Grades 11 and 12)** — and placed 503 items (102 from the pupil textbooks, 401 teacher-evidence items from the manuals and guides). The expansion map is wrong on both of its numbers (flag 1 — record-side, reported, not fixed by this merge): it said 6 student + 22 teacher in hand; the true set is 8 student + 24 teacher. The map missed three files that exist only as textbooks.tsv rows (the Grade 4 and Grade 6 full-year JICA pupil textbooks and the Grade 6 JICA manual) and did not know the Grade 2 SBC guide, which has a staging books.json row but no textbooks.tsv row and no push-state entry (flag 4). **Filing error (flag 2 — record-side): the git-tree copies recorded for the Grade 1 and Grade 2 JICA manuals are a different country’s book** — both TSV tree paths hold the identical file, a Mozambique Grade 1 teacher manual in Portuguese (“Manual do Professor, 1.ª classe”, 5,891,019 B / 216 pp, the same MD5 at both paths — the only byte-identical duplicate pair found in this hunt, and not a PNG book), which also sits in the release `mid-size-books-8` correctly named as a Mozambique book. The genuine PNG manuals were read from the GitHub release `full-quality-oversized-books`, whose assets match the staged sizes and checklist MD5s exactly; the push-state’s per-folder landing claims for those two files should be treated as unreliable until the tree paths are re-filed. Flag 3: the two manuals’ recorded education.gov.pg source URLs do not serve the staged pressings — both returned an HTML page during the read, and a later re-probe served a different pressing of the Grade 1 manual — so the staged pressings cannot be re-grabbed from the recorded source URLs. Flag 5: the Grade 9 SBC guide’s textbooks.tsv tree path 404s; the file was recovered by searching all release assets and matches books.json on size and pages. No Maths hides under other subject labels in the PNG records — all 156 staging books.json entries and all 163 textbooks.tsv rows were checked by subject, title and path. Link-only Maths not in hand and not read (it does not count against closure): the FODE distance course-books for Grades 7–12 Mathematics / Maths A / Maths G (unit PDFs) and the two OBE upper-secondary guides found 2026-11-10 (Advanced Mathematics TG, ISBN 978-9980-9924-8-2; General Mathematics TG). Book errors found on the read are taught corrected: the Grade 1 Textbook and its manual print “Subtracation” in the Chapter/Unit 12 title; the SBC guides print “Angels” for Angles in places, “Algebrain” for Algebraic (a Grade 11 guide’s Factorisation unit header) and “Orobability” for Probability (the Grade 9 guide’s strand table); the Grade 1 SBC guide’s Term-1 record prints “tenths” where tens is meant. The Grade 3 SBC programme has no Money topic where the JICA Grade 3 textbook carries one — a programme difference, not an error. Printed-page basis: JICA textbook pages cite the book’s own printed folio / contents page; JICA manuals cite the manual’s own printed contents page; SBC guides cite the guide’s printed programme/contents pages (the basis is stated in each book entry below); where a contents extraction was partly two-column-interleaved, the PDF page is given as well and marked “PDF p.”. Counting grain: one item per chapter (pupil textbooks), per unit (teacher’s manuals — the manual unit mirrors the textbook chapter of the same number and is counted once, as teacher evidence), and per named topic/unit in the SBC guides’ own programmes. The placements’ named exclusions are stocked nowhere: the end-of-book/year “Summary” chapters and units in the JICA textbooks and manuals (review apparatus), and the SBC guides’ front sections, assessment-record templates, glossaries/resources and integrated cross-subject project sections. Pupil-textbook rows are COUNTED normally; every manual and SBC guide row is teacher evidence — named in the countries line and in the rows, but outside the acceptance count. Into this lesson the placements stock 2 items — 0 pupil-textbook rows and 2 teacher-evidence rows — from Book T10 (*Mathematics Teacher Guide, Elementary Prep (SBC)*), Book T11 (*Mathematics Teacher Guide, Elementary One (SBC)*).

**Book T10:** *Mathematics Teacher Guide, Elementary Prep* (SBC teacher guide) — Papua New Guinea, Department of Education (NDoE), Standards Based Curriculum, 2015; 6,660,506 bytes, 206 PDF pp. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY — scripted daily lesson plans (printed pp. 23–184); the topics stocked here are the guide’s own, from its Planning and Programming tables (printed pp. 8–22) and its Assessment strand tables (printed pp. 185–187). No SBC pupil textbook is in hand at any grade.

**Book T11:** *Mathematics Teacher Guide, Elementary One* (SBC teacher guide) — Papua New Guinea, Department of Education (NDoE), Standards Based Curriculum, 2015; 8,102,294 bytes, 201 PDF pp. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY — scripted daily lesson plans; the topic list is the guide’s own, from its Assessment plan (printed pp. 182–183) and term assessment records. Book error noted on the read: the Term-1 record prints “tenths” where tens is meant in the place-value breakdown of 24.

## Sweden added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Sweden's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **13 files, 594 PDF pages**, of which **10 carry mathematics**: the national curriculum Lgr22, 2 SPSM pupil materials, and 7 SPSM teacher guides, notes and support publications. Sweden has **no state pupil textbook series** — textbook approval was abolished in 1991, Skolverket sets the curriculum (Lgr22), and the state producer SPSM (Specialpedagogiska skolmyndigheten) publishes special-needs materials — so the pupil teaching in hand is SPSM material for blind and deaf pupils, and nothing else: 13 pupil items (the Abakus exercise book and the Växelspelet money game), 81 teacher-evidence items and 79 curriculum-evidence items from Lgr22's Matematik kursplan, 173 items in all. The other 3 files in the read set prove on reading to carry no maths at all and are stocked nowhere: *Skrivmaterial för elever som behöver förstärkta linjer och rutor* is blank ruled/grid stationery (copying masters), the *ABC Skriva-Läsa* teacher guide is literacy only, and a generically named *Arbetsblad* worksheet (pharmacy vocabulary) was pulled in as a hidden-maths spot check and cleared negative. The expansion map is wrong on both axes (flag F4): it listed 8 files as 3 student + 5 teacher, but two of its eight are not Maths at all (F1: the Skriva-Läsa guide, filed in books.json under “Matematik; Svenska”, is a literacy guide — its only number-adjacent moment, drawing a numbered slip 2–5 to pick that many pictures (printed p. 6), is classroom apparatus; F3: the Skrivmaterial, counted as a student Maths book, is blank stationery), a third is the wrong kind (F2: *Räkna med mig*, filed as a student book, is a 105-page handbook for teachers of braille-using pupils — all 35 of its items are teacher evidence), and four maths-bearing files were missed by the map entirely — Lgr22 itself (its section 5.5 is the full Matematik syllabus), the *Arbeta med språkstörning i matematikundervisningen* support publication (filed under generic special education), *Växelspelet* (filed under “Verklighetsuppfattning”) and the *Jag handlar* teacher guide (filed under home-economics subjects). Flags F1–F4 are record/map-side: they are reported here and carried in this section; the records themselves are not edited — merges never edit the records, and no maths/books/sweden.md filing exists. The books' own errors are carried corrected: F5 — both Abakus volumes misprint their print ISBNs with a “987-” prefix (Övningsbok “987-91-28-11602-2”, Handledning “987-91-28-11600-8”; each book's PDF ISBN carries the correct 978- prefix); F6 — *Räkna med mig*'s ISBN is printed malformed as “98-91-28-10386-2”; F7 — the Abakus Handledning's division chapter (printed p. 45) says the pupil must master “multiplikation” where the sense requires division, taught corrected on the division row it touches; F8 — a box in *Räkna med mig* (printed p. 38) misnames the Abakus handbook as “Räkna med mig – handledning”. The placements' record items are stocked nowhere: R1 (Lgr22's assessment criteria, printed pp. 60–63 — they restate the central content as standards and add no content beyond the items), R2 (the Abakus Handledning's exercise sets and maths-card copying masters), R3 (the Arbeta publication's overview material), R4 (*Räkna med mig*'s apparatus), R5 (the Diamanterna card game itself is a physical product, not a book, and is not in hand — only its one-page SPSM note is) and R6 (the *Jag handlar* pupil book is recorded only as a sample that was never staged as a secured file; only its teacher guide is in hand). One basis note: the Övningsbok's multiplication and division exercise pages are abacus diagrams with no printed numerals, so those two placements rest on the book's contents page, section headings and introduction, exactly as the placements state. Sweden's class for Maths is **PARTIAL (evidence-heavy)**: every in-hand Maths-bearing file has been read and stocked, but 160 of its 173 items are teacher or curriculum evidence and its pupil side is special-needs material only. Into this lesson the placements stock 4 items: 4 teacher evidence. No Sweden pupil item stocks into this lesson, so Sweden is added to the countries line above and named in the rows below as evidence only, and is NOT added to the acceptance count, on the standing evidence-only precedent.

**Book 5:** *Lärarhandledning till 123-Räkna* (teacher guide) — Sweden, SPSM; 2,096,260 bytes, 17 PDF pp. (printed pp. 1–16), MD5 283165891421e043d9bf7bf74c05a94f. Guide to *123-Räkna*, SPSM's sign-language digital läromedel for deaf pupils, förskoleklass–åk 3 (special school to åk 4), built on Skolverket's “Hitta matematiken” mapping material and the national assessment support for number sense in åk 1–3; the guide quotes the Lspec11 maths syllabus (printed pp. 10–11). TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY.

**Book 9:** *Arbeta med språkstörning i matematikundervisningen* (support publication) — Sweden, SPSM, 2021, ISBN 978-91-28-00992-8; 764,290 bytes, 26 PDF pp., MD5 60b56c2eb928a6983dceccca12e03797. SPSM support publication on the language of mathematics for pupils with developmental language disorder. It teaches no pupil maths directly; the items placed from it are the concrete maths content it names, demonstrates or uses as worked material, placed as teacher evidence. Its overview material — the catalogue of known difficulty points (printed p. 4), its concrete-versus-abstract discussion (p. 9) and its general support catalogue (pp. 15, 17) — is record item R3, stocked nowhere. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY.

| Lesson part | Where it comes from |

| Comparison language taught through stories: more/most, fewer/fewest, bigger/biggest, smaller/smallest, equal numbers (123-Räkna, the Sago island) | Sweden — *Lärarhandledning till 123-Räkna* (teacher guide) — teacher evidence only, the guide (printed pp. 1–16) |
| Number-guessing games driven by more-than/fewer-than questions, ranges 1–10 and 1–20 (123-Räkna) | Sweden — *Lärarhandledning till 123-Räkna* (teacher guide) — teacher evidence only, the guide (printed pp. 1–16) |

| Symbol and digit confusions to guard against: > and < swapped, the digits 6 and 9, spoken sju/sjuttio and tretton/tretio, and digit transpositions such as 27 read as 72 | Sweden — *Arbeta med språkstörning i matematikundervisningen* (support publication) — teacher evidence only, printed p. 8 |

Sweden enters this lesson as evidence only: it is named in the countries line and in the rows above, but it is NOT added to the acceptance count — no Sweden pupil item stocks into this lesson, and its rows here are teacher-guide and curriculum evidence (the standing evidence-only precedent). ## Switzerland added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Switzerland's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **8 files / 6 works, and no pupil textbook among them**: the three PER brochures *Mathématiques et Sciences de la nature / Sciences humaines et sociales*, Cycles 1, 2 and 3 (CIIP, Version 3.0, août 2024), whose Mathématiques parts are placed here; the Ticino *Piano di studio della scuola dell'obbligo ticinese* (DECS, edizione perfezionata 2022), whose Matematica chapter is placed here; the *Lehrplan 21 Graubünden* (Fassung vom 15.03.2016), whose Mathematik plan is placed here — one work in three filed language editions (German, Romansh, Italian), read in full in the German edition, verified identical in structure and pagination in the other two, and cited once; and one teacher guide, *MaMa — Linee guida del progetto MaMa: Ambito Numeri e calcolo* (DECS/CeRDD, 2021), whose didactic rulings are placed here as teacher evidence — 425 items in all (364 curriculum evidence + 61 teacher-guide evidence, 0 pupil). The expansion map's kind split is WRONG (record-level; the records themselves are not edited — merges never edit the records): it recorded "3 student + 1 teacher", but the three "student" files are the PER **curriculum** brochures mislabelled — **Switzerland has 0 pupil Maths textbooks in hand**, and its prescribed pupil series (Schweizer Zahlenbuch, Mathematik Primarstufe / Sekundarstufe I (LMVZ), Mathwelt, Mathbuch, MER Mathématiques 1–11, Ticino's Base Matematica 1–4) are commercial and licence-gated, not in hand and not read. Further maths was found hiding under "all subjects" labels: the Ticino Piano and the Lehrplan 21 Graubünden are all-subjects volumes carrying full mathematics chapters. Every grabbed file was verified SHA-256 byte-exact against its books.json record. Checked in hand and excluded with reasons, stocked nowhere: the three PER *Aperçus des contenus* (one example-grain maths page each — record items R1–R3), the PER *Présentation générale* (framework structure only), the PER *Éducation numérique* brochure (the computer-science curriculum; mathematics appears only as cross-links — a separate school subject) and the four Vaud *<DÉ>CODAGE* digital-education teacher manuals (contents pages of all four read: Science informatique / Médias / Usages only — a separate school subject); inside the PER brochures, the Sciences de la nature and Sciences humaines et sociales parts are other subjects, the *Modélisation* objectives are process apparatus whose content is carried by the per-theme problem-solving items, and the Lexiques are apparatus; inside the Ticino volume, every chapter other than Matematica is another subject and the process/attitude competence targets carry no subject content; inside the Lehrplan 21 volumes, every subject other than Mathematik is out of scope and the Einführung/Gesamtüberblick chapters are structure, not content; MaMa §§1 and 3 are project apparatus, and §2's quoted Ticino targets are placed at the Ticino items, not double-counted (record item R4). One within-document inconsistency is flagged so it is not stocked: the PER Cycle 1 *Aperçus des contenus* claims Cycle 1 pupils "calculate lengths (perimeter of a rectangle), areas, volumes (cube)" — the governing Cycle 1 brochure requires only comparison and measurement with an étalon (items 46–55), and perimeter/area/volume calculation begins in Cycle 2 (items 158–160); the brochure governs, the Aperçu's Cycle 1 sentence is an overstatement, and it is NOT stocked as a Cycle 1 requirement (record item R1). One document error is called out by a source itself and taught corrected: the MaMa guide rules that there is no "dissociative property" of addition — splitting an addend and associating (16 + 19 = 16 + 10 + 9) is the associative property, and textbooks that name it otherwise are wrong (item 378); the guide's other flagged classroom errors are misconceptions it warns against, not errors it commits. No Liechtenstein file is judged here: Liechtenstein teaches from Switzerland's books, but no file is filed under Liechtenstein in any record. Printed-page basis: PER brochures, Ticino Piano and Lehrplan 21 — printed folio = PDF page − 2; MaMa — printed page = PDF page. Switzerland's class for Maths is **PARTIAL (evidence-only)**: every in-hand Maths-bearing file has been read and stocked, but all 425 of its items are curriculum or teacher-guide evidence and no pupil textbook is in hand. Into this lesson the placements stock 6 items (5 curriculum evidence and 1 teacher evidence) from Book 1, Book 2 and Book 5. Switzerland is added to the countries line and named in the rows below as evidence only, and is NOT added to the acceptance count, on the standing evidence-only precedent.

Printed folio = PDF p. − 2. Only the Mathématiques part (printed pp. 13–21) is placed here; the Sciences de la nature and Sciences humaines et sociales parts of the same brochure are other subjects. Filed in the records as a student book — wrong: it is a curriculum brochure. CURRICULUM EVIDENCE ONLY — the brochure states what must be taught and teaches nothing itself; its rows are worded as the requirements they are and are counted in no lesson's taught-in figure.

Printed p. = PDF p.
