## Timor-Leste added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Timor-Leste's whole in-hand library from the PDFs — **5 files**, of which **2 carry mathematics**: the Grade 5 pupil exercise book *Livru Ezersísiu Matemátika 5* and the Matemátika section (printed pp. 318–511) of *Livru Planu Lisaun Klase 3 Períodu 2*, the combined all-subject Grade 3 Period-2 lesson-plan volume. The other three files were checked in full and carry no maths teaching: *Livru Dalan Tetun ba Klase 5* (a Tetum grammar book — number words appear once, as one of eight grammatical word-classes), *Livru Planu Lisaun Klase 6 Períodu 2* (Natural and Social Science plans only; its four “matemátika” mentions sit inside one density lesson) and *Um Mundo de Histórias 6º ano* (a Portuguese reader; maths appears only inside stories). The expansion map's “1 student + 0 teacher” split counts only the books filed as Maths and repeats a filing error — **flag F1** (record-side: reported here, not fixed; merges never edit the records, and no `maths/books/timor-leste.md` filing exists): the Grade 3 volume is filed as the “Ciências Naturais Grade 3 Teacher Guide”, claiming Natural Science plans from p. 10 and Social Science plans from p. 468, but its own Índise (printed p. 3) lists all nine basic-education components and puts Matemátika at p. 318, Siénsia Naturál at p. 512 and Siénsia Sosiál at p. 624 — the mis-filing hid the country's only in-hand Maths teacher evidence. **Flag F2** (record gap): `textbooks.tsv` carries only 3 Timor-Leste rows — the two Grade 5 books, including the Maths exercise book, sit in `books.json`, the push state and the GitHub repo tree with no TSV row. Everything else Timor-Leste Maths is link-only and not in hand — the Matemática G1–G4 student books, the G10–G12 student manuals, the 2024 new-curriculum Grade 7 set and the Learning Passport book courses — and is cited nowhere. The placements number **177 items: 83 pupil-workbook items and 94 teacher-evidence items** (33 from full printed lesson plans; 61 from the period plan table alone, each marked “(plan table only)”). The workbook is a genuine pupil teaching book — 77 numbered exercise sets (Ezersísiu 1–77) plus two front-matter reading tables — but it practises and tests; it does not present topics first (its letter to teachers states the exercises follow the annual plan and lesson-plan sequence), and it is not the main Grade 5 textbook. The guide's Matemátika section is **Period 2 only** — the Period 1 and Period 3 volumes are not in hand, so Grade 3 coverage here is one period of three. The books' own errors are taught corrected: **E1** — the workbook's census table heads its second column “2005”, but its figures are the 2010 census and its questions ask about 2010; **E2** — Ezersísiu 70 is a reprint of Ezersísiu 68, counted once (as item 70); **E3** — Ezersísiu 60's fourth prism has equal dimensions in the Tetum text and different dimensions in the Portuguese (the Tetum governs); **E4** — the guide's Lisaun 51 plan tells the teacher to write “6 x 1 = 1” (read 6 x 1 = 6, as the page's own explanation gives); **E5** — the guide's introduction states 2 formal formative assessments per period while the plan schedules 6 (assessments are exclusions either way); **E6** — ten blank plan-table content cells are continuation/test rows, folded into their parent items, and lesson 84's body settles it as the fraction–decimal relation lesson. The placements' named exclusions are stocked nowhere: the workbook's covers, imprint, forewords, letters and “do not write in this book” notice; the guide's discipline introduction and assessment guidance (printed pp. 326–328); its 22 consolidation lessons, 6 formal-assessment lessons and 5 project lessons (no new content stated for any of them); blank continuation/test rows; homework and in-lesson revision steps; and the Grade 3 volume's other eight subject sections. Counting grain: pupil book — one item per distinct practised skill; teacher guide — one item per distinct taught content in the period plan. Timor-Leste's class for Maths is **COURSES-CLOSED** — a pupil-workbook set on the Peru pattern: the workbook's rows count, the guide's rows are teacher evidence and count nowhere. Into this lesson the placements stock 3 items: 0 pupil-workbook + 3 teacher evidence. Every Timor-Leste row in this lesson is teacher evidence, so Timor-Leste does not count here.

**Book 1:** *Livru Planu Lisaun Klase 3 Períodu 2* (teacher guide — the combined all-subject Grade 3 Period-2 lesson-plan volume; its Matemátika section only) — Ministério da Educação de Timor-Leste; 15,881,272 bytes, 829 PDF pp., MD5 18b0a4e0d55af5f56868c556dc9893f2. Its Índise (printed p. 3) lists all nine basic-education components; the Matemátika section (printed pp. 318–511) was read in full — full 50-minute lesson plans (objective, materials, timed teaching steps; the MAT3.x codes are the national curriculum's Grade 3 outcomes) plus the period plan table at printed pp. 318–325. Printed page = PDF page. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY — its rows are named in a lesson but counted in no taught-in figure, and the section is Period 2 only. Filed in the library records as the “Ciências Naturais Grade 3 Teacher Guide” — the filing error F1 carried in the intro above.

## Samoa added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Samoa's whole in-hand Maths library from the PDFs — **9 files: 8 pupil textbooks + 1 curriculum statement, 0 teacher guides** — and the expansion map's “7 student Maths books, 0 teacher” count is **wrong**: the map missed the *Mathematics Year 12* typeset edition, staged in the records as a supposed workbook (“full-year-wk”) but proved from its title page and interior to be the complete MESC Year 12 pupil textbook (Units 1–8 + answers), and it missed the kind of the *Primary Curriculum Statement, Years 1–8*, which is a curriculum document, not a pupil book. The library's enumerated slot for the separate 113 MB scanned Year 12 edition is NOT in hand (live 404; only a truncated 1 MB archive stub exists) — that edition is cited nowhere and the slot stays a library-side matter; the complete typeset Year 12 pupil textbook in hand is the book read. No teacher guide for Maths exists in the Samoa set, and no Maths content was found hiding under another subject label. The eight pupil books are real pupil teaching books — lessons, worked examples and exercises addressed to the learner — so Samoa's pupil rows **count** in a lesson's taught-in figure. The curriculum statement (CMAD, MESC, January 2013) states what is REQUIRED to be taught in Samoa primary schools and teaches nothing itself: its rows are **curriculum evidence only** and count in no taught-in figure — Samoa has no primary pupil textbooks in hand, so every primary (Years 1–8) Samoa row in the course is curriculum evidence. Counting grain: one item per distinct teaching section of a pupil book (the book's own numbered sections and their named subsections), and one item per coded learning outcome (NR / PA / DA / MS / SG / WM codes) of the curriculum statement; worked examples and exercise sets inside a section are part of that section's item, not separate items. The books' own errors are taught corrected: Year 10 Book 2's §7.1 heading prints “Drawing an Englargement” (for Enlargement); Year 11 Book 2's unit opener prints “Dealing with Angles Larger than 900” (for 90°); Year 13's contents lists chapter 2 as “Linear Inequations” while the chapter's printed title is “Linear Equations” and it teaches both — placed under the printed title. Named exclusions, stocked nowhere: the answers sections of every pupil book; Year 13's “Problems and Investigations” end-of-chapter sections, its Glossary/Index and its formula Appendix; calculator-keystroke asides (the mathematics is placed, the keystrokes are not); the curriculum statement's strand progression tables (printed pp. 81–120), which restate its coded outcomes; and Year 12's Unit 8 project unit, placed as one item. Into this lesson the placements stock 1 items: 1 pupil.

## Turkmenistan added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Turkmenistan's whole filed in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **5 pupil files = 3 pupil works, plus 1 teacher guide** (6 documents, 840 PDF pages in all) — and the expansion map's "3 student + 0 teacher" kind split is **wrong** (**flag F1**: the map counts one file per grade/track slot; it misses the second parts of both Turkmen works and the teacher guide entirely — any plan sized on "3 books" under-sizes the read by about 2.5×). The pupil works are the complete Turkmen-medium Grade 1 and Grade 2 courses — *Matematika* Parts 1 and 2 for each grade (Atabaýew & Öwezowa, 3rd editions, 2019/2020) — and Part 1 only of the Russian-medium Grade 1 course (Rulieva, Chernikova, Mavlyanova & Satdykova, 4th ed. 2022), a separate work by a separate author team, not a translation of the Turkmen pair; the two Grade 1 Turkmen books are scans with no text layer and were read from page renders. The one teacher book, N. Açilowa's *Başlangyç synplarda matematikany okatmagyň usulyýeti* (Methodology of Teaching Mathematics in the Primary Classes, Ylym, 2015), is a teacher-training study guide: it teaches NO pupil content, so its rows are **teacher evidence only** and count in no lesson's taught-in figure; the five pupil books are real pupil textbooks, recommended by the Ministry of Education of Turkmenistan and served by the Ministry's own E-kitaphana, so their rows **count**. **Flag F2** (record-side: reported here, not fixed; merges never edit the records, and no `maths/books/turkmenistan.md` filing exists): a `books.json` note claims the Russian Grade 1 Part 2 "is E-kitaphana post 507" — post 507 is in fact **Grade 2 Russian Mathematics Part 1** (per the enumeration table and the grid match), no Grade 1 Russian Part 2 post exists in the catalogue, and that Part 2 is recorded nowhere as found; post 507 must not be double-counted as both a Grade 1 and a Grade 2 book, and the Russian Grade 1 work stands in hand as Part 1 only. **Flag F3**: the filing notes for these books rested on samples ("every-fifth-page verification", contents lists not yet verified); this full re-read confirms the sampled claims spot-checked — e.g. the kilogram section of Grade 1 (Turkmen) Part 2 does sit at printed pp. 63–64 — and supplies the missing contents lists for both Grade 2 parts (Part 1 contents at PDF p. 103; Part 2 at PDF pp. 118–119); no sampled content claim checked was found false. **Flag F4**: the map's "2 link-only" figure cannot be confirmed and looks far too low — the banked E-kitaphana grid match carries Maths links for the Grade 2 Russian pair and for Grades 3–9 Mathematics, Algebra and Geometry, plus a Grade 5 methodology guide; every one of those is link-only, not in hand and cited nowhere, and everything beyond Grade 2 is outside this set. Byte-identical duplicate posts are cited once, on the filed copy (post 403 = the Grade 1 Part 2 file; post 412 = the Grade 2 Part 1 file; post 418 = the Grade 2 Part 2 file; post 576 = the teacher guide's post 555 file), and every pupil file was pulled from its GitHub release asset at the exact recorded byte size (reconcile sweep #8 lists 23/23 Turkmenistan entries secured). Counting grain: one item per distinct teaching section of a book (its contents-list section, or a headed teaching block where the contents list runs broader), with consolidation/revision sections counted once per book-part; pure exercise pages inside a section are apparatus of that section's item. In every book in this set the printed folio equals the PDF page. One reading note, carried exactly: in the Grade 1 (Turkmen) Part 1 book's type, the empty answer box prints as a small square — in the PDF font layer it can appear as «*» (e.g. the comparing exercises "7 * 6" mean "put <, > or = between 7 and 6") — it is NOT a multiplication sign, and multiplication is not taught in that book. Not stocked as separate rows, exactly as the placements direct: the Grade 2 Part 1 money item 14 (manat notes and coins inside addition/subtraction stories at printed pp. 50, 61 — context only, no money section is taught in that book, and the placements count it inside items 11–13), and the four consolidation/revision items the placements assign to no lesson (Grade 2 Part 1 item 17, Grade 2 Part 2 item 42, Grade 1 Part 1 item 71, Grade 1 Russian Part 1 item 125) — practice over sections already stocked, no new content. Turkmenistan's class for Maths is **COURSES-CLOSED** on the as-filed set. Into this lesson the placements stock 8 items, all of them pupil.

## Tunisia added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Tunisia's whole in-hand Maths set from the file — exactly 1 book, a teacher's guide, and no pupil textbook: the *Mathematics — Teacher's Book, Year 1 of Basic Education* (CNP code 502107P00). The expansion map is CORRECT for the in-hand set (0 student + 1 teacher), with one title refinement carried everywhere: the library/checklist title says “grades 1–2” because the checklist's one teacher-guide row spans both volumes, but this file is the **Year 1 volume only** — its cover names Year 1 alone, and the Year 2 volume is the separate file 502205P00. The guide is the only mathematics row for Tunisia in `textbooks.tsv` (75 Tunisia rows: Arabic, English, French, philosophy, Spanish and this one) and the only mathematics entry in any staging `books.json`; no Maths hides under another subject label and there is no “all subjects” Tunisia volume. The book was grabbed byte-exact through the authenticated GitHub API (9,205,715 bytes, 184 PDF pages, MD5 d017ae08ae7c35d335757b0fb1ef747f — matching the library's verified record for the complete Wayback capture of 502107P00; staging holds no Tunisia PDF on disk) and read in full from rendered pages — the PDF's text layer is glyph-encoded (extraction is mojibake) and Arabic OCR returned nothing on these watermarked scans; the diagonal CNP watermark does not hide body text. Printed folio = PDF page throughout. Counting grain: one item per distinct concept, skill or teaching point the guide itself teaches or explains, at the grain the guide presents it; lesson-plan conduct prose, activity scripts and assessment apparatus are not separate items unless they carry a distinct mathematical teaching point. Where the guide itself separates teacher background theory from what Year 1 pupils actually do, the item says so — the teacher-background items (16, 17, 28, 57, 58, 75, 76 and 77) are placed as teacher background, exactly as the placements word them. The placements' named exclusions are stocked nowhere: the life-skills chapter (printed pp. 55–67 — general life-skills/21st-century-skills pedagogy, no mathematics content); the error-remediation procedure, didactic-contract discussion and remediation planning (printed pp. 45–50 passim — teaching apparatus; the content-bearing error examples are placed as items); the model lesson plans (printed pp. 78–132) beyond the points placed from the distributed sequence; and the pupil-facing integration and problem-solving worksheets of Part Three (printed pp. 158–179), practice material enacting the placed concepts and introducing none the reference or sequence sections do not already teach. Record items R1–R5 are apparatus or exam evidence, stocked nowhere as teaching items: R1 the assessment typology — diagnostic before new learning, formative during, certifying at a period's or year's end (printed pp. 41–42); R2 the six period evaluations (printed pp. 134–152) — exam evidence only; R3 the closing Arabic–French glossary of mathematical terms (printed pp. 154–157); R4 the guide's statement (printed p. 158) that the pupil textbook is held to at most 120 pages, so the extra integration and problem-solving situations are printed in this guide instead — the pupil book and this guide are designed as one package; R5 the bibliography (printed pp. 180–182). Set boundary: the pupil textbooks for Years 1, 2 and 6 (102105P01, 102105P02, 102210P00, 102609P00), the Year 2 volume of this teacher's guide (502205P00) and all later-year najahni/exercice.tn material are exact-link only — recorded links, never downloaded, in no `books.json`, not in hand and not read. Tunisia is **TEACHER-GUIDE EVIDENCE ONLY** in every lesson: every item is worded as what the guide teaches or explains, Tunisia is named in the countries line and in the rows below, and it is added to NO lesson's acceptance count, on the Ghana precedent. Tunisia's class for Maths is **PARTIAL (teacher-evidence-only)**. Into this lesson the placements stock 5 teacher-evidence items from Book 1.

**Book 1:** *Mathematics — Teacher's Book, Year 1 of Basic Education* (رياضيات — كتاب المعلم، السنة الأولى من التعليم الأساسي) — Tunisia, Ministry of Education, Centre National Pédagogique (CNP), code 502107P00; authors Sami Al-Jazi, Awatef Al-Marwani, Salwa Achour (inspectors); 9,205,715 bytes, 184 PDF pages, MD5 d017ae08ae7c35d335757b0fb1ef747f. Teacher's guide — TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY. Printed folio = PDF page. Read in full (printed pp. 1–184) from rendered pages; the PDF text layer is glyph-encoded. Structure: Part One — reference (background notes, problem-solving, assessment, mathematical language); Part Two — practical (period maps, model lessons, period evaluations); Part Three — resources (bibliography, assessment situations, Arabic–French glossary).

| Lesson part | Where it comes from |
|---|---|
| In Year 1 the official programme names none of the mapping kinds — only the correspondence relation is used, comparing two sets element-by-element with the words 'more than', 'less than' and 'as many as' | Tunisia — *Mathematics — Teacher's Book, Year 1 of Basic Education* (teacher's guide) — teacher evidence, printed pp. 13–16 |
| Correspondence built through movement and sharing games — children changing places inside a ring at a signal; counting towels against children and toothbrushes against teeth; giving out pencils so each child takes one of every colour — the country's own concrete route into one-to-one correspondence | Tunisia — *Mathematics — Teacher's Book, Year 1 of Basic Education* (teacher's guide) — teacher evidence, printed pp. 13–16 |
| Error source: a child of 6–7 can judge which of two sets has more by the space the elements take up rather than by counting them — comparing sets by extent instead of by number | Tunisia — *Mathematics — Teacher's Book, Year 1 of Basic Education* (teacher's guide) — teacher evidence, printed pp. 45–50 |
| The comparison signs <, > and = are taught as their own lesson between the numbers 0 to 9 | Tunisia — *Mathematics — Teacher's Book, Year 1 of Basic Education* (teacher's guide) — teacher evidence, printed pp. 69–76 |
| Numbers lying between two known numbers are found and written, as their own mental-arithmetic topic | Tunisia — *Mathematics — Teacher's Book, Year 1 of Basic Education* (teacher's guide) — teacher evidence, printed pp. 69–76 |

Tunisia enters this lesson as teacher-guide evidence only: it is named in the countries line and in the rows above, but it is NOT added to the acceptance count above — Tunisia's only in-hand Maths book is a teacher's guide and no Tunisia pupil textbook is in hand (the standing Ghana evidence precedent). Tunisia's class for Maths is PARTIAL (teacher-evidence-only): the single in-hand book is read and stocked in full, and it advances only when a Tunisia pupil textbook — the Year 1, Year 2 or Year 6 pupil books, today exact-link only — lands in hand and is read.

## Vanuatu added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Vanuatu's whole in-hand Maths set from the file — exactly 1 book, a teacher guide, and no pupil textbook: *Tija Gaed blong Matematiks Yia 3* (Mathematics Teacher Guide, Year 3, Bislama, MoET 2017). The expansion map is CORRECT for the in-hand set (0 student + 1 teacher = 1); the TSV's other distinct Vanuatu books are the Language & Communication Y2, Living in Community Y2 and Science Y3 teacher guides and the Bislama Spelling Dictionary Y2 — no Maths hides under another subject label and there is no "all subjects" Vanuatu volume in hand. The staging PDF was deleted after pushing (the pusher's normal pattern); the read copy was grabbed from the recorded source via its banked Wayback capture (2019-11-17 `id_` replay of education.gov.vu/docs/textbooks/Matematiks_2017.pdf) and is byte-identical to the record: 11,818,504 bytes, 317 PDF pages, SHA-256 2b7aa33d2ee0a81d9512e4f141c208bfc04a0abdf6d5bf77d58356a31cf22ec6, matching the staging `books.json` hash exactly. Printed folio = PDF page \u2212 1 throughout (verified on the outcomes section, weekly lesson footers and the glossary). Counting grain: one item per distinct teaching topic in the Year 3 column of the guide's Learning Outcomes & Indicators tables (printed pp. 12\u201322), worded from the outcome and its indicators and confirmed against the weekly lessons that teach it (Terms 1\u20133, printed pp. 23\u2013294); items taught in a weekly lesson but stated in no Year 3 outcome are marked (D). Assessment questions, resources lists and vocabulary boxes are apparatus for the counted items, not separate items. Record items R1\u2013R4 are stocked nowhere as teaching items: R1 the trilingual Bislama\u2013English\u2013French glossary of Year 3 mathematics words (printed pp. 313\u2013316) — teacher-reference apparatus; R2 the photocopiable appendix masters (printed pp. 295\u2013312) — they carry no teaching topic not already counted; R3 the programme facts from the introduction (printed pp. 4\u20136 — five strands, ten sub-strands, seven hours of Year 3 mathematics a week under the National Timetable Policy 2013); R4 the Beginning / Year 1 / Year 2 columns of the outcomes tables — those are the earlier years' guides, which are NOT in hand for Maths, and nothing from them is counted. The placements' named exclusions are stocked nowhere: the introduction apart from R3 (printed pp. 4\u201310 — lesson planning, literacy and inclusive-education guidance, teaching craft only); the Beginning/Year 1/Year 2 progression columns; the weekly vocabulary boxes, resources lists and assessment apparatus; and syllabus/timetable exposition carried as R3 only. Book errors E1\u2013E5 are carried taught corrected where they affect stocking: E1 — printed p. 15 abbreviates millilitres "(mm)" (mm is millimetres; millilitres are mL) and writes litres "(l)"; E2 — the Term 3 Week 3 length-conversion lesson (printed p. 233) is headed with the operations outcome NO.3.V1 though it is measurement; E3 — the money lesson (printed p. 202) cites its outcome as "M3.V1" for MM.3.V1; E4 — the Year 3 probability outcome code is printed "CPD.3.V1" (printed p. 21) against the CDP codes of the other columns; E5 — template slips in the term overviews (printed pp. 23, 210), none affecting content. Set boundary: the ministry `/docs/curriculum/` Maths Student's Books Y3\u2013Y6 (English stream), the French-stream Maths 3e\u20136e Ann\u00e9e, the Mathematics Teacher's Guide (Year 4) and the Kindergarten Guide (2013) are exact-URL candidates only — never downloaded, in no `books.json`, not in the push-state, not in hand and not read. Vanuatu is **TEACHER-GUIDE EVIDENCE ONLY** in every lesson: every item is worded as what the guide teaches, Vanuatu is named in the countries line and in the rows below, and it is added to NO lesson's acceptance count, on the Ghana precedent. Vanuatu's class for Maths is **PARTIAL (evidence-only)**. Into this lesson the placements stock 1 teacher-evidence item(s) from Book 1.

**Book 1:** *Tija Gaed blong Matematiks Yia 3* (Mathematics Teacher Guide, Year 3, Bislama) \u2014 Vanuatu, Ministry of Education and Training (MoET, Ministri blong Edukesen mo Trening, Ripablik blong Vanuatu), 2017, developed with Australian/NZ aid support (Education Support Program); 11,818,504 bytes, 317 PDF pages, SHA-256 2b7aa33d2ee0a81d9512e4f141c208bfc04a0abdf6d5bf77d58356a31cf22ec6. Teacher guide \u2014 TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY. Printed folio = PDF page \u2212 1. Read in full: outcomes and indicators printed pp. 12\u201322; weekly lessons Term 1 printed pp. 23\u2013124, Term 2 printed pp. 125\u2013209, Term 3 printed pp. 210\u2013294.

## Trinidad and Tobago added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Trinidad and Tobago's whole in-hand Maths-bearing set from the files — **9 files, all curriculum/teacher evidence, 0 pupil textbooks**: the dedicated *Primary School Curriculum — Curriculum Guides: Mathematics (Infant 1 – Standard 5)* (MoE, Curriculum Planning and Development Division, 2013), read in full; seven per-level all-subject Primary Curriculum Guides (Infants 1, Infants 2, Standards 1–5), recorded in every record under the plain “Curriculum Guide” label, whose Mathematics sections are the **same curriculum text** as the dedicated guide's section for that level — verified file by file (normalised-text sequence similarity 0.9973 Infants 1, 0.9977 Infants 2, 0.9975 Standard 2, 0.9986 Standard 5; word-multiset containment 1.0000 Standard 1 and 0.9948 Standard 4, whose per-level productions extract in a different column order; Standard 3's scanned guide verified by OCR — rationale “What Is Mathematics About?” at PDF p. 60, “MATHEMATICS: STANDARD 3” tables at PDF pp. 70/80/90, three content spot-checks matching) — so they are **cited once, through the dedicated guide** (placements Record items R1–R7), never double-counted; and the *National Early Childhood Care and Education Curriculum Guide* (ages 3–4), whose mathematics strand is placed separately (items 245–249) because the dedicated guide starts at Infant 1. The expansion map's set picture (0 student + 1 teacher) is wrong as a set description: the dedicated guide it counted is real, but the true in-hand set is these 9 files, and **no pupil Maths textbook for Trinidad and Tobago exists in hand under any label** (all pupil textbooks are commercial — STP Caribbean Mathematics and others; none is held). Every document in the set is a Ministry curriculum guide — a document that states what is REQUIRED to be taught; it teaches nothing itself — so every Trinidad and Tobago item is a curriculum REQUIREMENT item, worded as curriculum evidence. Counting grain: one item per numbered Outcome in the guide's OUTCOMES column, with the placements' stated folds (a bare “solve problems involving X” outcome that restates its own section is folded into that section's item; per-measure vocabulary outcomes are folded into their measure's item; each strand's closing Language outcome is its own item). The placements' named exclusions are stocked nowhere: the dedicated guide's front matter (PDF pp. 1–26 — contents, National Curriculum Framework prose, Subject Rationale); the Dispositions columns of every table (attitudes and values — apparatus, never items); the per-level guides' non-Mathematics subjects; the 17 Forms 1–3 guides staged at grade-08 (ELA, ICT, Spanish, Integrated Science, Social Sciences, VAPA, Visual Arts, Dance, Drama, Music, PE, HFLE, Technology Education — checked book by book; none is a Mathematics book; the Social Sciences staging anchor was labelled “Teacher's Guide” in its source bundle, but the file itself is the Social Sciences Curriculum Guide — a records mislabel, noted, not a Maths item); and the ECCE guide's non-mathematics strands and apparatus-level mentions (§5.9.9 equipment list; §5.9.16 a number line 1–10 in a materials list). Book errors are read corrected: in Standard 4's Number Patterns table the Outcomes-column wording of Outcome 5 is displaced across Outcomes 4 and 6 in print — the requirement was read from its elaborations (the items 183/184 boundary) and is placed corrected; a Standard 4 Decimals conversion elaboration cross-cites code [1.1.5] where [1.1.15] is meant, read as 1.1.15; and fraction glyphs (halves, quarters, fifths, tenths) are dropped at several points in the dedicated guide's text layer — the fractions were read from context and the surrounding elaborations. Set boundary: the *NCSE Mathematics Curriculum Guide (Forms 1–3)* is **link-only, not in hand** — exact links known (official MoE blob copy 2,900,122 B; IBE/UNESCO mirror 2,587,043 B) but the file was never staged or downloaded, so it was not read and is cited nowhere; the NCSE Mathematics *Teacher's* Guide has no public PDF on any route checked by the library fronts (provisional negative). Trinidad and Tobago is **CURRICULUM EVIDENCE ONLY** in every lesson: every item is worded as what the guide requires, Trinidad and Tobago is named in the countries line and in the rows below, and it is added to NO lesson's acceptance count, on the Barbados/Ghana precedent. Trinidad and Tobago's class for Maths is **PARTIAL (evidence-only)**. Into this lesson the placements stock 2 curriculum-evidence item(s) from Book 1.

**Book 1:** *Primary School Curriculum — Curriculum Guides: Mathematics (Infant 1 – Standard 5)* — Trinidad and Tobago, Ministry of Education, Curriculum Planning and Development Division (CPDD), 2013; 3,422,006 bytes, 264 PDF pp., MD5 f45d565e6dfe0b2a8a37cf400198a21e. Curriculum guide — CURRICULUM EVIDENCE ONLY: it states what is required to be taught and teaches nothing itself. Printed page = PDF page − 3 throughout. Read in full (items 1–244: Infant 1 printed pp. 24–44; Infant 2 printed pp. 45–72; Standard 1 printed pp. 73–115; Standard 2 printed pp. 116–161; Standard 3 printed pp. 162–199; Standard 4 printed pp. 200–239; Standard 5 printed pp. 240–261).
